{"forms":[{"form":"Trojan tests","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A test of binarity. A test to determine if a two-body system is a primary and satellite, or a binary system. A test to determine if the L4 and L5 Trojan points (Lagrange points) are stable or not. If it is determined they are stable, the system is that of a primary and satellite. (where one component is at least 25x times more/less massive than the other) If the points are not stable, then the system is that of two components in a binary relationship. (where the two components have less than 25x the difference in mass with the other)"],"topics":["astronomy","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A test of binarity. A test to determine if a two-body system is a primary and satellite, or a binary system. A test to determine if the L4 and L5 Trojan points (Lagrange points) are stable or not. If it is determined they are stable, the system is that of a primary and satellite. (where one component is at least 25x times more/less massive than the other) If the points are not stable, then the system is that of two components in a binary relationship. (where the two components have less than 25x the difference in mass with the other)","The mass ratio of 1:25 or 25:1 diving line for the existence of stable Trojan points, marking the divide between binary systems and primary-satellite systems."],"topics":["astronomy","natural-sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"L4-L5 instability test"},{"word":"L4-L5 stability test"},{"word":"Lagrange instability test"},{"word":"Lagrange point instability test"},{"word":"Lagrange points instability test"},{"word":"Lagrange points stability test"},{"word":"Lagrange point stability test"},{"word":"Lagrange points test"},{"word":"Lagrange point test"},{"word":"Lagrange stability test"},{"word":"Lagrange test"},{"word":"Lagrangian instability test"},{"word":"Lagrangian point instability test"},{"word":"Lagrangian points instability test"},{"word":"Lagrangian points stability test"},{"word":"Lagrangian point stability test"},{"word":"Lagrangian points test"},{"word":"Lagrangian point test"},{"word":"Lagrangian stability test"},{"word":"Trojan instability test"},{"word":"Trojan point instability test"},{"word":"Trojan points instability test"},{"word":"Trojan points stability test"},{"word":"Trojan point stability test"},{"word":"Trojan points test"},{"word":"Trojan point test"},{"word":"Trojan stability test"}],"upstream_line":10819663,"upstream_offset":26095875270,"upstream_record_sha256":"221643b612b82399c5fd2daed31d10880ff3ed2a0cb2292afe22bc66441482cb","word":"Trojan test"}
